{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"noo","tags":["alternative","Geordie","Scotland","dialectal"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"adv","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Now I am six.","type":"example"},{"text":"Stop that now, Jimmy!","type":"example"},{"text":"I could have been Secretary of State now if I hadn’t left politics five years ago.","type":"example"},{"text":"We can’t phone now – they will certainly have gone to bed.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["At the present time."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Now, we all want what is best for our children.","type":"example"},{"text":"Now, Jimmy, stop that.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to introduce a point, a qualification of what has previously been said, a remonstration or a rebuke."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Now I am ready.","type":"example"},{"text":"We all now want the latest toys for our children.","type":"example"},{"text":"We all want what is now best for our children.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Differently from the immediate past; differently from a more remote past or a possible future; differently from all other times."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Now he remembered why he had come.","type":"example"},{"text":"He now asked her whether she had made pudding.","type":"example"},{"text":"The pudding was now ready to be served.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["At the time reached within a narration."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Now listen, we must do something about this.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to indicate a context of urgency."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I always used to do my shopping now, to avoid the rush.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["At the present point of a recurring cycle or event."],"tags":["informal","not-comparable"]},{"glosses":["Very recently; not long ago; up to the present."],"raw_tags":["as but now"],"tags":["not-comparable","obsolete"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Now, you want to protect me.
